## Matched-error comparison

Common levels of `error_common`: 1.000e-2, 3.000e-3, 1.000e-3, 3.000e-4, 1.000e-4, 3.000e-5, 1.000e-5, 3.000e-6, 1.000e-6, 3.000e-7, 1.000e-7, 3.000e-8, 1.000e-8. At each level both arms' median wall time and RHS count are interpolated in log–log on their own successful points. `time_ratio = comparator / VigilODE` (above 1: VigilODE faster). Ratios exist only where both arms bracket the level; every other entry is `DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY`. Band-structured comparators are compared with `vigilode-banded`, the others with `vigilode`. Reference-implementation-only arms are excluded.

This section is the grid-clipped continuity comparison: the `vigilode` and `vigilode-banded` arms land a step on every one of the grid times, so their step size is capped by the grid spacing and the extra steps are inside every such time and RHS count, while every comparator steps freely and interpolates. The headline comparison with an endpoint-only VigilODE is in the next section. The table below gives, for every VigilODE row, the accepted steps and how many of them were clipped to a grid time.

## Endpoint-only VigilODE (dense output)

Headline comparison. The baseline is the facade `Rodas5p::solve` with `dense_output = true` and interior error control `WFilteredEnforce` (the product default); grid values come from `Solution::at`, so no step is clipped to a grid time. Band-structured comparators are compared with `vigilode-banded-dense`, the others with `vigilode-dense`. A dense row with output-clipped steps, or with a grid time the interpolant does not answer, has status `protocol-violation` and is kept; rows with that status: 0. Timing authority: diagnostic_only. Ratios across hosts are never compared.

## Rows

`error_common`: max over the grid of the WRMS error against the reference with weights `error_rtol (atol_scale + |y_ref|)`; `error_own`: the same with the run's own weights `rtol (atol_scale + |y_ref|)`, i.e. its own atol vector and rtol. Work counts are solver-reported (`—`: not reported; `clipped`: VigilODE steps shortened to land on a grid time); `rhs`, `jac`, `jvp` are the shared model's callback counts. Allocations: every heap allocation call of the run (`heap`) and its Rust subset (`rust`). Failed runs are kept with their work.

## Arms

| arm | fidelity | output interface | deviations from vendor defaults |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| vigilode-dense | solver-under-test | Rodas5p::solve with dense_output = true (free stepping), grid values from Solution::at(t_k); the driver that ran is recorded per row as native.driver_id | facade defaults except dense_output = true and Atol::Scalar(rtol * atol_scale); interior_error = WFilteredEnforce (the product default), a row with output_clipped_steps != 0, or with Solution::at(t_k) = None inside the span of a successful run, is a protocol-violation |
| vigilode-banded-dense | solver-under-test | Rodas5p::solve with dense_output = true (free stepping), grid values from Solution::at(t_k); the driver that ran is recorded per row as native.driver_id | facade defaults except dense_output = true and Atol::Scalar(rtol * atol_scale); interior_error = WFilteredEnforce (the product default), a row with output_clipped_steps != 0, or with Solution::at(t_k) = None inside the span of a successful run, is a protocol-violation |
| vigilode | solver-under-test | grid-clipped (continuity arm): OutputSchedule of the protected fast driver, each grid time is a step landing (steps clipped to it); the endpoint-only facade route is not used | driver called directly with the facade's endpoint-only route and defaults (h0 = 1e-3, max_steps = 100000) and an OutputSchedule over the grid; route equivalence is tested bit-for-bit at t1, grid landings add clipped steps that a free-stepping run would not take (grid-clipped continuity arm) |
| vigilode-banded | solver-under-test | grid-clipped (continuity arm): OutputSchedule of the protected fast driver, each grid time is a step landing (steps clipped to it); the endpoint-only facade route is not used | driver called directly with the facade's endpoint-only route and defaults (h0 = 1e-3, max_steps = 100000) and an OutputSchedule over the grid; route equivalence is tested bit-for-bit at t1, grid landings add clipped steps that a free-stepping run would not take (grid-clipped continuity arm) |
| diffsol-bdf-dense | production | OdeSolverMethod::solve_dense(t_eval) (BDF interpolation, stop time = last grid point) | Jacobian assembled by Diffsol from n JVPs of the shared analytic Jacobian (builder rhs_implicit; no coloring, the dense default) |
| diffsol-bdf-sparse | production | OdeSolverMethod::solve_dense(t_eval) (BDF interpolation, stop time = last grid point) | Jacobian assembled by Diffsol from coloured JVPs of the shared analytic Jacobian (sparsity detected by NaN propagation, the sparse default) |
| cvode-bdf-dense | production | CV_ONE_STEP loop + CVodeGetDky(t, 0) for every grid time inside the last step | one-step mode with a total cap of 100000 internal steps (CVODE's mxstep = 500 applies per CVode call and would not bind in one-step mode) |
| cvode-bdf-band | production | CV_ONE_STEP loop + CVodeGetDky(t, 0) for every grid time inside the last step | one-step mode with a total cap of 100000 internal steps (CVODE's mxstep = 500 applies per CVode call and would not bind in one-step mode) |
| dvode-mf21 | production | DVODE ITASK = 1 (interpolated output at TOUT) |  |
| dvode-mf24 | production | DVODE ITASK = 1 (interpolated output at TOUT) |  |
| radau5 | production | SOLOUT (IOUT = 1) + CONTR5 | initial H = 0 (the code then uses 1e-6) |
| radau5-band | production | SOLOUT (IOUT = 1) + CONTR5 | initial H = 0 (the code then uses 1e-6) |
| rodas | production | SOLOUT (IOUT = 1) + CONTRO | initial H = 0 (the code then uses 1e-6) |
| rodas-band | production | SOLOUT (IOUT = 1) + CONTRO | initial H = 0 (the code then uses 1e-6) |
